bout. League]] that took place on December 20, 2025, at the Sofitel Dome in [[Cotonou]], Benin.{{Cite web |date=2025-11-13 |title=PFL Africa Finals Full Card Set for December 20 in Cotonou, Benin |url=https://pflmma.com/news/pfl-africa-finals-full-card-set-for-december-20-in-cotonou-benin |access-date=2025-11-16 |website=Professional Fighters League |language=en-US}}{{Cite news |last=Akinbo |first=Peter |title=PFL Africa finale holds Dec 20 in Benin \n|url=https://punchng.com/pfl-africa-finale-holds-dec-20-in-benin/ |access-date=2025-11-16 |newspaper=[[The Punch]]|language=en}}{{Cite web |last=Kiewietz |first=Julian |date=2025-11-13 |title=PFL Africa 2025 Finals fight card confirmed: Africa\u2019s best set to make history marked the promotion's debut in Benin, which became the third country in the Africa region (after [[South Africa]] and [[Rwanda]]).{{Cite web |date=2025-07-21 |title=MMA: PFL Africa to Hold 2025 2025 PFL Africa Tournament in a heavyweight, welterweight, featherweight and bantamweight divisions.{{Cite web |last=Pendleton |first=Mike |title=Professional Fighters League Release Full Lineup for PFL Africa Finals \n|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Professional-Fighters-League-Release-Full-Lineup-for-PFL-Africa-Finals-199039 |access-date=2025-11-16 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\nAt the fight week, Karim Henni\u00e8ne withdrew from the event against Nkosi Ndebele due to an injury and was replaced by Boule Godogo.{{Cite web |last=Treese |first=Tyler |title=PFL Benin Weigh-in |access-date=2025-06-30 |website=Sherdog |language=en}} The promotion was originally scheduled [[PFL Paris 2025 (cancelled)|an event in France]] on May 23, but the event was cancelled and shifted to July 5 in Belgium instead.{{Cite web |last=Barry |first=Corral |date=2025-05-07 |title=Evra's MMA debut on hold as PFL Paris delayed |language=fr-FR}} However, Abdouraguimov withdrew from the event due to personal reasons and the bout was cancelled. [[ESPN Deportes]]\n|first_aired = {{start date|2009|4|3}}\n|last_aired = {{end date|2009|6|19}}\n}}\n\n'''2009 in Bellator MMA''' was the first installment of the [[Bellator Fighting Championships]] (Bellator FC)-produced series. It started broadcasting April 3, 2009, through an exclusive television agreement with [[ESPN Deportes]]. The episodes had a one-day tape delay.\n\n==Bellator 1==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=Bellator 1\n|image=\n|promotion=[[Bellator Fighting Championships]]\n|date=April 3, 2009\n|venue=[[Seminole Hard Rock Hotel & Casino Hollywood]]\n|city=[[Hollywood, Florida]], United States\n|attendance= 1,756\n|previousevent=\n|followingevent=[[Bellator 2]]\n}}\n\n'''''Bellator 1''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event by [[Bellator Fighting Championships]]. The event took place on Friday, April 3, 2009 at the [[Hard Rock Cafe|Seminole Hard Rock Hotel & Casino]] in [[Hollywood, Florida]].{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/events/BFC-Bellator-Fighting-Championships-1-9708|title=Bellator Fighting Championships 1|publisher=sherdog.com|access-date=2011-05-21}} It was broadcast nationally in the U.S. via tape-delay the following night on Saturday, April 4, 2009 through an exclusive television agreement with [[ESPN Deportes]].\n\nThe event featured quarter-final tournament bouts of the 2009 Featherweight (145 lb) and Lightweight (155 lb) Tournaments, along with a number of non-tournament bouts to be held in the organization's other weight divisions. All bouts were contested under the [[Unified Rules of Mixed Martial Arts]].\n\n'''Results'''\n\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Lightweight Quarterfinals}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n| Lightweight\n| [[Jorge [[mixed martial arts]] event by the promotion [[Bellator Fighting Championships]]. The event took place on Friday, April 10, 2009 at the [[Mohegan Sun]] in [[Uncasville, Connecticut]].{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/events/BFC-Bellator-Fighting-Championships-2-9754|title=Bellator Fighting Championships 2|publisher=sherdog.com|access-date=2011-05-21}} It was then broadcast nationally in the U.S. via tape-delay the following night on Saturday, April 11, 2009 through an exclusive television agreement with [[ESPN Deportes]].\n\n'''Background'''\n\nThe event featured the final quarter-final bouts of the 2009 Featherweight (145 lb) and Lightweight (155 lb) Tournaments, as well as, the quarter-final round of the Welterweight (170 lb) Tournament. A number of non-tournament bouts to be held in the organization's other weight divisions also took place. The evening's main event saw undefeated [[Henry Martinez (fighter)|Henry Martinez]] square off against rising star [[Wilson Reis]].{{cite web| url=http://mmamania.com/2009/03/06/wilson-reis-to-headline-second-bellator-event-at-mohegan-sun-on-april-10/#more-11409| title=Wilson Reis to headline second Bellator event at Mohegan Sun on April 10| 2009\n|venue=[[Lloyd Noble Center]]\n|city=[[Norman, Oklahoma]], United States\n|attendance= 4,297\n|previousevent=[[Bellator 2]]\n|followingevent=[[Bellator 4]]\n}}\n\n'''''Bellator 3''''' and '''''Bellator IV''''' were two episodes of the [[mixed martial arts]] promotion, [[Bellator Fighting Championships]], created from a single event. The event took place on Friday, April 17, 2009 at the [[Lloyd Noble Center]] in [[Norman, Oklahoma]].{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/events/BFC-Bellator-Fighting-Championships-3-4-9858|title=Bellator Fighting Championships 3 & 4|publisher=sherdog.com|access-date=2011-05-21}}\n\n'''Background'''\n\n'''''Bellator IV''''' was originally scheduled for [[Montreal]], however regulatory issues prevented the event from being held there. Each episode featured half of the bouts from the third Bellator event. ''Bellator III'' aired nationally in the U.S. via tape-delay the following night on Saturday, April 18, 2009 through an exclusive television agreement with [[ESPN Deportes]]. ''Bellator IV'' aired on Saturday, April 25, 2009.\n\n'''Results'''\n\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Middleweight Quarterfinals}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n| Middleweight\n| [[Hector Lombard]]\n| def.\n| by the [[Professional Fighters League]] that took place on December 13, 2025, at [[LDLC Arena]] in [[D\u00e9cines-Charpieu]], France.{{Cite web |date=2025-09-24 |title=Two World Title Fights to Headline PFL Lyon at LDLC Arena in Lyon, France on December 13 |url=https://pflmma.com/news/two-world-title-fights-to-headline-pfl-lyon-at-ldlc-arena-in-lyon-france-on-december-13-home-town-at-zenith-nantes-metropole |access-date=2025-09-24 |website=Professional Fighters League |language=en-US}}{{Cite web |last=Anderson |first=Jay |date=2025-09-24 |title=Ferreira vs. Nemkov, Cyborg vs. Collins Title Fights Announced for PFL Lyon |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2025/09/24/ferreira-nemkov-cyborg-collins-title-fights-announced-pfl-lyon/ |access-date=2025-09-24 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event marked the promotion's second visit to D\u00e9cines-Charpieu in the [[Metropolis of Lyon]] and first since [[PFL Europe 4 (2024)]] in December 2024.{{Cite web |date=2025-09-24 |title=PFL announces two huge title fights for PFL Lyon event |url=https://www.lowkickmma.com/pfl-lyon-huge-title-fights-announced/ |access-date=2025-09-24 |website=www.lowkickmma.com |language=en-US}}\n\nThe event was headlined by an inaugural [[List of Professional Fighters League champions#Heavyweight Championship|PFL Heavyweight Championship]] bout between former [[Bellator Light Heavyweight World Championship|Bellator Light Heavyweight World Champion]] [[Vadim Nemkov]] and [[2023 in Professional Fighters League#2023 PFL Heavyweight playoffs|2023 PFL Heavyweight Tournament]] winner [[Renan Ferreira (fighter)|Renan Ferreira]].{{Cite web |last=Hannoun |first=Farah |title=Vadim Nemkov-Renan Ferreira, Cris Cyborg-Sara Collins title fights headline PFL Lyon |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/story/sports/pfl/2025/09/24/pfl-lyon-vadim-nemkov-cris-cyborg-title-fights-headline/86325922007/ |access-date=2025-09-24 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nIn addition, a [[List of Professional Fighters League champions#Women's Featherweight Championship|PFL Women's Featherweight Championship]] bout between former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Women's Featherweight Championship|Strikeforce]], [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Featherweight Championship|Invicta FC]], [[List of UFC champions#Women's Featherweight Championship|UFC]] and [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Women's Featherweight World Championship|Bellator Women's Featherweight World Champion]] [[Cris Cyborg]] and [[Sara Collins (fighter)|Sara Collins]] served as the co-main event.{{Cite web |last=Holland |first=Jesse |date=2025-09-24 |title=Cyborg returns! PFL Lyon topped by two title fights for Dec. 13, including Vadim Nemkov vs. Renan Ferreira |url=https://www.mmamania.com/professional-fighters-league-pfl/391288/cyborg-returns-pfl-lyon-two-title-fights-dec-13-vadim-nemkov-vs-renan-ferreira |access-date=2025-09-24 |website=MMAmania.com |language=en}}\n\nThe event also included the finals of 2025 PFL Europe Tournament in a lightweight and bantamweight divisions.{{Cite web |last=Critchfield |first=Tristen |title=Two Title Fights Headline PFL Europe Finals on Dec. 13 in France |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Two-Title-Fights-Headline-PFL-Europe-Finals-on-Dec-13-in-France-198462 |access-date=2025-09-24 |website=Sherdog
